# Version log

|---|---|---|
| T-Rex style | 1.1 (StripeEye) | Preset B cartoon proportions + cream throat→belly stripe + square eye with highlight |
| Rig | 1.3 | 17 joints; knee + ankle joints (legs split thigh / shin / foot); 1.3: thigh + shin 30% longer (hip 9.68 studs up) |
| StarterCharacter | 1.2 | Player copy of the T-Rex; HRP re-aimed to the dino's forward; scripts in StarterCharacterScripts; 1.2: rig 1.3 legs, HipHeight 4.96 |
| IdleAnimation | 1.25 | Client animator: idle + walk/run/creep blend + crouched stance + head turn into turns + roar layer + bite layer (local prediction, hit-stop); running turns no longer speed the legs up; 1.13: tail on its own (half-speed) phase; 1.14: head lead layer (HeadLeadPose); 1.15: leap layer (JumpPose), JumpEnabled dinos only; 1.16: pounce layer (PouncePose), PounceEnabled dinos only; head lead suppressed while it runs; 1.17: move direction in the body frame (MoveDir) handed to WalkPose 3.4, eases to forward below 2 studs/s; WalkDirectional = false keeps forward-only; 1.18: bite layer gets BiteDip / BiteDipPredicted; 1.19: ram layer (RamPose, RamEnabled dinos; local state on the owner, server stamps elsewhere; head lead suppressed) + stun flailing layer on every dino while StunnedUntil; 1.20: eat / drink layer (EatPose 1.0) from the replicated SurvivalAction, blended at EatBlendRate 6/s after the jump / pounce layers, idle look + head lead suppressed; mouth reach: the lowest Head + Jaw point is steered to the lake surface − 0.3 (drink) or the carcass top (eat) by an extra neck dip (EatReach, −35..40°); 1.21: eat / drink follow SurvivalServer 1.1 phases (lowering → in → rising, timed from SurvivalPhaseAt / SurvivalEnterTime / SurvivalRiseTime, so the mouth is in exactly when the refill starts; interrupted → fades at 10/s); reach solved by forward kinematics (bisection) for the in target (water − 1.0 / carcass top − 0.6; never below the ground under the head) and a hover target; neck aims at the carcass (±35°); dead pose layer (DeadPose 1.0) last while Health ≤ 0 or DummyState "ko" (DeadPoseState / DeadPoseBlend); 1.22: X eyes (DeadPose.markEyes) while the dead pose is on, undone when up again (DeadEyes); 1.23: bite layer passes BiteAimYaw / BiteAimYawPredicted to BitePose 1.2 (head turns toward the aimed target); 1.24: re-fit on scale (DINO-6) — joint rest frames × new/old Model scale, leg measurement scaled, knee IK rebuilt; verified at 0.4–1.0 (client Neck C0 = server, feet on the ground while walking at 0.4); 1.25: refitScale skips the template copy in StarterCharacterScripts (it errored every frame: "GetScale is not a valid member of StarterCharacterScripts") |
| IdlePose | 1.4 | Breathing loop + occasional head turn; 1.4: tail sway on its own 2 s loop (IdleTailPeriod) |
| WalkPose | 3.4 | Distance-driven walk/run/creep, 2-joint knee IK, planted feet, run gait blend, creep blend + crouched stance; 3.2: separate tail phase (WalkTailRate 0.5); 3.3: stride stretch (Walk/Run/CreepStrideStretch, default 1) + RunNeckDrop (default 0); 3.4: foot path along the move direction — backward = reverse steps, strafe = side steps by a hip roll (ankle rolls back, foot flat), capped at WalkSideStepReach × hip offset (default 1) |
| BitePose | 1.2 | Quick bite: wind-up 0.15 s (rear + gape) → strike 0.10 s (lunge down, jaws shut) → recover 0.30 s; head thrash on hit; 1.1: dip — neck swings down by (BiteDip − BiteLunge) to reach low targets (BiteDipMax 60); 1.2: optional aim yaw (BiteAimMax 45): neck 60 % / head 40 %, 60 % in the wind-up, full at the strike |
| TurnPose | 1.0 | Neck + head yaw and tilt into turns while moving (TurnHead* attributes) |
| HeadLeadPose | 1.0 | Neck + head turn toward the input direction before the body (up to 45°, moving and standing; off during roar / bite) |
| TurnLimit | 1.1 | Owner client: body turn capped at TurnLimitRate 180 °/s (AutoRotate off), movement follows the body (wide arcs); input from the Input Action System MoveAction; sends the turn still to go; 1.1: TurnLimitMode "aim" (set by ShoulderCam) — body turns toward the camera yaw at the same rate, movement camera-relative (A/D strafe) |
| StatusBadges | 1.1 | Every client: badges above a dino while roar-debuffed — blue ▼ DEF (damage taken up), red ▲ SPD (speed up); timer bar + pop on apply/refresh; 1.1: SPD on NPCs too |
| TurnServer | 1.0 | Validates the turn still to go (number, ≤30/s), sets replicated TurnError for other clients' head lead |
| RoarPose | 1.0 | Wind-up → roar (jaw 40°, head shake) → recover, upper body only |
| RoarFX | 1.1 | Sound + forward-shooting sound-wave rings from the mouth + camera shake |
| RoarServer | 1.1 | Validates roar requests, cooldown, server-time stamp; 1.1: roar debuff — 90° cone, RoarRadius 30, timed with the rings: +25% damage taken, +10% speed, 8 s, refresh (no stacking) |

pinned + KO roll onto their side; KO → removed after 2.5 s, respawn after 10 s; sets player Hidden (inside a bush); 1.1: hiding spots also read from DinoIsland.TallGrass; 1.2: dipped bites (T-Rex bot can bite raptors), fling guard (velocity caps), raptor bots pounce + latch onto bigger dinos (8 + 4/0.5 s, 6.7 s, PounceFX), shake-off (water leap / ram stun 3 s), T-Rex bot runs for a tree or water when a player raptor is on it; latched raptor bots stop colliding (fix: a T-Rex player carrying one was slowed to ~6 studs/s); 1.4: raptors in 2 packs of 3 (markers 1–2; marker 3 unused), leader wanders, followers in formation behind; pack members keep ≥40 studs from any T-Rex (flee 35, hold to 55, wander goals away from him) and never target T-Rex players; a T-Rex player's bite on a member gives that pack a 20 s grudge: flank spots (left / right of the torso, behind the tail), side bites and latches never on head / neck / jaw (latch onto torso / tail only), dash out after each bite, ≤2 latched per pack; water avoided (wander / follow / flee / chase / flank goals cut at the shore, detour along the shore around lakes, no pounce into water, leap-off toward land, walk out if in water); fix: ram toss clamp used the old ±230 island → raptors tossed far out were dragged up to hundreds of studs; now the island limit (lands 15 studs from where it was); 1.5: T-Rex bot shake run goes to the nearest water (was a point 222 studs out = inland on the current island); T-Rex bot wanders on dry land and walks out of water when not chasing / shaking (BotTRexAvoidWater); bots stuck in a lake (10-stud terraces, no jump) scramble up the banks (anchored hop toward dry land); 1.6: size-aware (growth, DINO-9) — a Hatchling T-Rex player (GrowthStage 1) is not avoided by packs and may be hunted by them; bot damage on a player with a growth stage capped at 0.4 × MaxHealth per bite / latch land / latch tick; BotSizeAwareEnabled false (or growth off) = 1.5 behaviour; 1.7: Hidden from GrassCover 1.0 (head height vs grass height, creeping edge 4 studs; toggle AmbushRulesEnabled, false = the v1.6 box test), HiddenReason / HiddenTop / HiddenGrassHeight / CoverHint, AmbushPatches counts (DINO-26); 1.8: 9 roaming bots in 5 groups (Roam1/Roam2 raptor packs of 3, Roam3/Roam4 lone raptors as packs of 1, Roam5 T-Rex) from runtime markers in DinoIsland.RoamMarkers, moving home via BotRoam 1.0 (stay 20–40 s, next spot 250–650 studs, ≥ 250 from spawn), respawn ≥ 150 from players, sidestep / regroup around trees, toggle BotRoamEnabled (DINO-29) |

| ClimbTest | 1.12 | Kepu 2026-10-31 "Fix the bug" (grab at a leaning palm's base threw the Herrera to the crown). Cause: trunk-end and trunk-ahead probes are single sphere casts along the facing; a leaning trunk drifts off that line (CoconutPalm_7 from +-x: mantle after 0.15-0.27 s, y 13 -> 53; CoconutPalm_24 from +z under 1.11: y 18 -> 62.7 at 0.32 s). Fix: (1) a missed head probe counts as the trunk end only when the probe point is at / above the climbed tree's trunk top (switch ClimbTrunkTopEnabled; ClimbTrunkTopY, ClimbEndCheck); (2) while on a trunk, trunkAhead and the hold pull come from the climbed tree's trunk axis at body height plus the lean feed-forward (switch ClimbTrunkFollowEnabled; ClimbFollow). Studio after: Palm_7 4 sides and Palm_24 3 sides climb the whole trunk (1.1-1.7 s) and mantle only at the top (y 53 / 63); Palm_219 and JungleTree_91 mantle onto their canopy as before; Palm_24 up / cling y 24.6 / down / cling y 17.7 / JUMP off OK. Just allow it to climb through the leaves"; "jump off the tree top it bounces me backwards like a glitch". (1) Climb through the leaves (ClimbThroughEnabled): the mantle no longer teleports; the body climbs on in a straight line at ClimbSpeed x 1.2 to the stand point (pulled inside the landing pad by half a body + 1 stud), with local NoCollisionConstraints HRP x the tree's proxies and every colliding body part x the tree's trunk, removed on arrival; on arrival held still sideways (ClimbSettleVelocity) until the stick is let go / a jump / 1 s. (2) JUMP on a tree top = the normal leap (ClimbTopJumpEnabled): no hopOffTop, JumpSuppressed only on the trunk. Diagnostics ClimbThrough, ClimbReason through / throughDone / throughTimeout. ## Open decisions
- Skill points per level-up.
- Per-class walk/run speeds (~~current 16 / 28 are test values~~ current test values: T-Rex walk 20 / run 44 / creep 10 / roar 4 (TRexModelData 1.12); raptor walk 30 / run 52 (RaptorModelData 1.0); final per-class values still open — Dev Hub decision 3).
- ~~Roar of Terror effect area: forward cone (matches the waves) or circle.~~ Resolved: forward cone, RoarConeAngle 90, RoarRadius 30 (RoarServer 1.1).
- ~~Stamina for sprinting.~~ Resolved: StaminaServer 1.0 (run 15 s, refill 5 s, exhausted at 0 until 25%). Per-class stamina rules still open — Dev Hub decision 3.
- Creep gameplay effect (stealth / detection radius).
- Bite: damage to players / health system, ~~stamina cost~~ (resolved: StaminaBiteCost 10, StaminaServer 1.0), final chomp sound (current BiteSoundId 108790087307118 is a Creator Store placeholder).

| MainMenu | 1.11 | Bug (Kepu 2026-10-31 "the stun breaker keeps showing up on the back of the herrera even when not equipped ... Only what is equipped should be visible"). Root cause: the menu preview is a copy of the live character, re-taken only when the preview key changes; the key and the ChildAdded / ChildRemoved watchers looked at "Mecha_*" children only, and the Herrera's parts are "HPart_<id>" (HerreraPartsServer), so equipping / unequipping a Herrera part in the menu never re-copied (the menu Herrera kept the Stun Breaker it wore when the copy was taken) and the plain view kept HPart_* pieces. The live character itself was correct (Studio: slot breaker -> HPart_breaker built, slot cleared -> removed). Audit of all dinos with every slot empty (test layer on): raptor shows only Mecha_Blade (Blade Claws, passive), pachy shows nothing, Herrera shows HPart_ironclaw / _plating / _siphon (the stat rows, passive by the pass-3 pick "keep looks"); every other part is built only while slotted; all raptor / pachy parts are named Mecha_*. Fix: isMechaPart(name) = "Mecha_*" or "HPart_*" for the key, the watchers and the plain view. Switch MainMenuHPartsEnabled. | Lab | 1.10 | Bug (Kepu 2026-11-01, Lab screenshot: "It has the stun breaker appearing on my herrera even tho it's not equipped. I thought we addressed this"). Root cause: MainMenu 1.11 (v295) fixed the stale preview in the main menu only; the Lab's stage (rebuildStage) has the same logic: the copy of the live character is re-taken only when its key changes, and the key plus the ChildAdded / ChildRemoved watchers counted "Mecha_*" children only. Herrera parts are "HPart_<id>" (HerreraPartsServer), so equipping / unequipping a Herrera part never re-copied the stage (it kept the Stun Breaker from when the copy was taken), and the SKINS plain view kept HPart_* pieces. Fix (same as MainMenu 1.11): isMechaPart(name) = "Mecha_*" or "HPart_*" for the stage key, the watchers and the plain view. Switch LabHPartsEnabled (default true). Repo source 76482/1463991527 (length/hash). NOT INSTALLED: Studio not reachable from this session (computer not linked); install + Studio check (Herrera: slot breaker -> stage shows it, unequip -> stage drops it) when linked. |

| SkillEffects 1.36 | — | Bug (Kepu 2026-11-02 15:08: "Leap doesn't do crap. Tail grab doesn't do anything. The stomp has no cool down barely any visuals. The mortar is unusable"). Root cause: SkillEffects' effectsOf passed a dino only for the Herrera (1.26); a teno got ST.effects(steps) with no dino, and SkillTree's teno blocks (1.49 parts, 1.50 stats part 2, 1.52 stomp drain) fill their fields only when dino == "Teno". So since SkillEffects 1.33 every teno part was Enabled with every number 0: range 0 (grab "noTarget", stomp hit nothing, tiny rings), cooldown 0 (server floor 0.5 s: "no cool down"), leap charges 0 (always "cooldown"), mortar range 0 < min 15 (math.clamp error). Earlier Studio checks forced the character attributes and fired the remotes directly, so they never went through effectsOf. Fix: a teno (character Species, else DinoSpecies) reads ST.effects(steps, "Teno"); switch SkillEffectsTenoDinoEnabled. Kepu's build: 47 fields change, all teno-part fields; no other field changes. Hash 67688 / 344245196 (= repo). Studio Play (PC, Sandbox, Kepu's own save, real key presses, nothing forced): TenoGrabRange 12 / Cooldown 16, MortarRange 60, StompRange 20 / Cooldown 15, LeapDist 55, Charges 2; V leap moved 59 studs (1 charge left); C stomp hit 1 bot, second press "cooldown" 12.9 s; R grab caught and flung a raptor (8 damage, 13 s cooldown); T mortar landed at the crosshair 58.8 studs out. No new console errors. Stats part 2 (Survivor / Heavy Tail / Front Claws / Armored Hide) had the same cause and now read as the teno's too. Published to Mecha Dino Arena - Arena 2026-11-02 15:14 (Kepu: "Publish"). |

| therizino_kit_options mockup 0.3 (picked: combo A Reaper Sweeps, jump J1 Diving Rake) | — | Kepu 2026-11-02 19:32 on v0.2: "You didn't change anything other than the fucking lighting ... The animation itself looks like fucking shit". Root cause found: v0.2's poses used the wrong joint axes (its "r" is roll, not pitch: every lean / raise was a sideways roll) and its claws never reached the target. v0.3 (docs/mockups/therizino_kit_options_v0.3.html): axes measured on the rig (p = pitch, y = yaw, r = roll); every arm key pose solved from hand / claw-tip targets around the target (key spec tools/therizino_kit_keys_v0.3.py); feet planted with the crouch from the legs; the body follows the keys through sub-stepped springs (arms snap with a little overshoot, neck / head / tail lag and settle); swept claw contact, so the hits land where the claws touch (contacts at 0.33 / 0.74 / 1.34 s, leap 1.03 s); the finisher's claws shake while reared up; camera framed per move. Video docs/reference/therizino/therizino_kit_v0.3.mp4 (real speed + slow motion). B / C / J2 dropped (A + J1 picked). No game code changed. |

| TheriPose 1.2 | — | MDA-206 (Kepu 2026-11-03 "You open it" -> Studio session). Sidewinder drag fixed. Root cause (measured): the run layer multiplied RUN_B onto WalkPose's run gait (RunArmTuck 35 + arm swing, RunLean, neck / tail motion); RUN_B was solved without them, so the right claws rode behind and up (HRP-local (-14.7, -3.6, 6.4) predicted with the tuck = Play (-15, -2.7..-4.7, 6..8)). Fixes: (1) while running, every joint but the legs blends from WalkPose to rest by the run weight before the run layer (legs keep WalkPose's world pose); (2) when the wrist cannot reach, the solver rolls ArmR (+-25); (3) the claw solver re-measures the ground at every step (bends moved tips 2-4 studs; on a 14-degree ramp that was up to 0.94 studs into the slope). Switches TheriRunOwnUpperEnabled / TheriDragShoulderEnabled / TheriClawRecastEnabled (model attributes, default true); TheriRunPoseEnabled default true again. Installed 32567/540915073 = repo. Play on a flat test floor and a 14-degree ramp (client-only ZZ_ parts, Play only): lowest right tip flat 0.12-0.29 (255 frames), ramp 0.01-0.29 (315), never under the ground, above 0.5 never; leap mid-run: 36 air frames, no claws under the ground; left claws tucked (6.2, -5.8, -18) vs mockup (6.3, -4.5, -18.1). Not published; waits for Kepu to see the run. |

| BotServer 1.25, TenoMovesServer 1.7, TenoBotMotion 1.0, TenoMovesFX 1.5, IdleAnimation 1.41, TenoPose 1.9 | — | MDA-215..219, plan docs/plans/TENO_TAIL_GRAB_v1.0.md, mockup docs/mockups/teno_tail_grab_v0.1.html (Kepu 2026-11-03 17:42: "It lags the dinos when I toss them ... have the tail actually grab the Dino and throw it ... in front ... Same for behind. Also fix the jump cause when I land on dinos they start to glitch"; 18:03 "Option A for both" = grab A Over the Top + leap L1 Shove Clear). Root causes (Studio client logs): the server stepped the anchored bot every Heartbeat (clients saw ~30 Hz steps, the carry never moved); the grab's stun made BotServer lie the bot down at once (sank 0.3 s) and get it up at the grab spot (~75 studs back, under the ground); bots kept the Humanoid FallingDown state after every get-up (root sat HipHeight low ~1.6 s, then popped); many bots were owned by the nearest player; the JUMP landing check ignored dinos (stood on the dino's back, slam only after sliding off). Fixes: BotServer 1.25 grab hold (TenoGrabHeldUntil), get-up where it is, ownership guard, FallingDown/Ragdoll off at get-up; TenoMovesServer 1.7 Over-the-Top timeline (front: catch 0.70 throw 1.35 -> thrown behind; back: catch 0.45 throw 1.10 -> thrown ahead; flight 0.85), server never moves the bot until the landing, Bitable off while held; leap lands on a dino (ray + 0.15 s look-ahead), slam measured from the stand-on point, the dino under the body shoved sideways clear of it (0.28 s); TenoBotMotion 1.0 draws carry / flight / shove every frame on every client; TenoPose 1.9 + IdleAnimation 1.41 tail IK (clamp reaches the dino, over the back, whip, follow-through); TenoMovesFX 1.5 flash at the catch. Switches: BotGrabHoldEnabled, BotDownRefreshEnabled, BotOwnerGuardEnabled, BotStandFixEnabled, TenoMovesGrabOverTopEnabled, TenoMovesLeapOnDinoEnabled, TenoMovesLeapShoveEnabled, TenoBotMotionEnabled, TenoMovesFXGrabCatchEnabled, TenoGrabIKEnabled. Hashes (= repo): BotServer 152242/686337272, TenoMovesServer 74936/381771084, TenoBotMotion 9593/120699732, TenoMovesFX 31518/982888505, IdleAnimation 81884/17438573, TenoPose 40057/717068074. Studio Play (PC, teno, raptor bots held still for the test): back grab - clamp reaches the bot (IK miss 0.2-1.3 studs), carried over the back, thrown ahead, bot drawn every frame, lands 77 studs ahead, lies and gets up there standing (no snap-back, no sink); front grab - tail arcs over the back onto the bot, lifts it, throws it behind; leap onto a bot - TenoLeapLandedOn dino, hits 1, shoves 1, the bot slid 6 studs clear, the teno dropped to the ground, the bot got up standing; a plain stun: get-up stays at stand height (was 604.4 for 1.6 s). Screens: the back grab (bot held high by the clamp, then flying ahead) seen in the Play viewport. Not checked: a player as the grab / shove target (no second player), touch / gamepad (no HUD change), the therizino's JUMP landing on a dino (same code path). Not published. |

| DiloAttackServer 1.2 | — | Kepu 2026-11-04 11:53 "sometimes it appears to fly right through the Dino in front of me"; fix pick "Hits where it lands". Cause (Studio repro): the dart flies to the locked target's root as it was at the spit; on arrival 1.0 needed that root within DART_TOLERANCE 4 x scale (8 studs) of To, so a target moving during the flight missed while its body was still on To (bot moved 10 studs: To 1.4 studs from its hitboxes, root 10.0 studs away, no hit). The slower dart (DiloModelData 1.3 speed 45, v410) made flights 33% longer. Fix: arrival also hits when the locked target's nearest hitbox is within DART_BODY_REACH 1.5 x scale (3 studs) of To; DiloHitPoint = To for those. Switch DiloAttackBodyHitEnabled; diagnostics DiloDartBodyHits (script), DiloDartHitRule (character). Studio: shift 10 -> "body" hit (6 + venom), shift 30 -> "miss", shift 0 -> "root" hit. | IdleAnimation 1.44, TheriPose 1.9 | — | Kepu 2026-11-04 18:03 "when I'm latched and I do my jump animation it doesn't show shit it just shows the jump with the damage happening" ("Raptor latched on my therizino"). Root cause: the jump layer's ground probe (Raycast down from the root, filter {model}) hit the latched raptor riding under the torso, so JumpGroundDist went negative (-8.1) and the phase went crouch -> push -> none, never "air" (no Diving Rake roll). 1.44: jumpProbe() also excludes other players' characters, DinoIsland.Bots, DinoFX_Local, DinoFX, TenoFX (switch: model attribute JumpProbeIgnoreDinosEnabled, false = the 1.43 {model} filter); TheriPose 1.9: the air-phase rake probe uses the same exclusion. Studio: raptor riding 7.5 studs under the torso -> crouch -> air (roll 0 -> 358 deg) -> land. Not published (ships with the next "Publish"). |

Causes (Studio): DinoHitboxes 1.2 tail hitboxes collide, so the landing slam / mortar coil pushed the tail into the ground and lifted the body 2.4–3.2 studs; the slam waited ~0.2 s for the server's TenoLeapLandedAt; the grab clamp point was the old Clamp Tail's (-2.6) while the v460 Pincer Tail grips at -3.7. Fixes: TenoPoseFix (owner client) turns Hitbox_Tail* CanCollide off while TenoPoseState is tland / tmortar / tgrab / tleap and stamps TenoLocalLandAt at the local touchdown; TenoPose starts the owner's tland from it; TenoPartsServer sets TenoGrabClampX -3.7 with the Pincer Tail; TenoPose IK + TenoBotMotion hold point use it. Studio Play: jump slam at touchdown (was +0.22 s), body rise 0.00 (was 2.4–3.2); mortar rise 0.00 (was 3.16); back-grab raptor 0.3–2.2 studs from the jaws (was 4–7); challenge rise 0.01. Open (animation pass): front grab reach (bot pulled to the tail), throw IK miss up to 5.8. Not published. |

The bot latch = the player latch: BotLatchMaxTime 2.5 s, land BotLatchLandDamage 8, BotLatchTickDamage 5 every BotLatchTickInterval 0.5 s = 33 (1.27: 6.7 s, 8 + 13 x 4 = 60, end checked before the tick). The tick due at the end lands first (PounceServer 1.16's order). Switch BotLatchBalanceEnabled (script, default true, read at each latch; false = 1.27). Diagnostic: bot BotLatchTicks. pounce_latch_sim 1.1 ports the bot step: PASS 20/20 (60 / 30 Hz, mid-frame latch, 50 ms hitch, Teno Stomp cut, 1.27 before row). |

| TenoMovesServer 1.18, StunClient 1.4, MechaButtons 1.43, AbilityBar 1.37 | — | Kepu 2026-11-09 after v589 "we need to fix the teno tail grab. There's no indicator when a Dino is out of range. Found: (1) a grabbed player was never thrown: TenoMovesServer stuns it at the catch and stamps the toss 0.6 s later at the throw, StunClient 1.3 only looks for a toss when the stun starts (Studio replay: rolled over in place, 0 studs). StunClient 1.4 late toss (StunLateTossEnabled): a toss newer than the stun's start starts the flight from where the body is (replay: tossed 45 studs, StunLateTosses 1; a ShakeOffServer-style toss unchanged, no second toss). (2) the grab's ground ray hit tree canopies: 2.6% of 4,000 random landing rays stopped on a canopy / frond 35-47 studs up, and a bot standing under a tree got its lift measured to the canopy above it (clamped to 1 -> sunk). TenoMovesServer 1.18 (TenoGrabSkipTreesEnabled) leaves DinoIsland.Trees out of the grab's lift and landing rays (Studio: a throw under JungleTree_75 landed on the ground Y 26, the 1.17 ray hit Canopy3 at Y 73.8; TenoMovesGrabTreeSkips 1). (3) indicator: TenoMovesServer 1.18 sets TenoGrabInRange / TenoGrabInRangeTarget every 0.1 s from the press's own pick (TenoGrabRangeHintEnabled); AbilityBar 1.37 (PC tile) and MechaButtons 1.43 (touch button or its pull label) show the ready TAIL GRAB green (rgb 110, 235, 130, stroke 3.5) while true and dimmed while false. Studio: hint false -> press "noTarget", hint true -> press "ok" (same pick); PC tile lit green in a screen capture; touch (MechaButtonsForceTouch hook, removed after) Slot_2 pull label "TAIL GRAB" green at 0, MechaButtonsGrabHint "lit". Carry frame trace (bot vs tail tip every frame): the bot follows the jaws, the only big steps are the tail's own whip at the throw (<= 4.4 studs per frame).
